## Luckiest animal today – Pig (កុរ) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color brown (ត្នោត) · best time 05:00–07:00 · lucky numbers 8 · 22 · 283 · 2588 · 56894 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a premium-quality 3D animated fantasy character of a chubby, adorable golden piglet with big happy eyes and rosy cheeks, the Pig of the Chinese zodiac, inspired by the charm and enchanting visual appeal of classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films. The pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune and a happy home: a happy, warm and generous expression and pose that show this. Body in creamy white-gold with shining engraved gold patterns, pink inner ears or rosy cheeks, polished gold textures, luminous highlights and delicate pink and gold accents. It sits on a round golden pedestal inside a large glowing pink lotus floating on a still pond. Behind it, a huge glowing full moon with a soft silver-gold halo, a deep indigo starry sky, a softly illuminated Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with warm golden lights, palm tree silhouettes, small floating lanterns, glowing lotus flowers on the water, gentle moonbeams falling on the pig, magical sparkles and soft silver-and-gold reflections. Cinematic lighting, soft atmospheric glow, realistic metallic reflections, beautiful depth of field, professional animated-movie-quality rendering. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around the character. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cropped body parts.

## 60-second video – Facebook & TikTok (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video about the Pig, today's luckiest animal: 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Pig as ingredients. Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. 1. Identity

4. Challenge

★ = used to open this video. Why: Specific and true (only the day's animal has a Great Day), creates curiosity, and every viewer wants to know if it is their sign.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Pig, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Pig as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Pig's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Pig and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Pig? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Pig? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Wednesday, 28 October 2026, is a Pig day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Pig. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Generous and warm-hearted, the Pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune, and a happy home. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Pig are warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing. Pigs value comfort, kindness, and honest relationships.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Pig? Your luck is strong today. Stay happy, warm and generous. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
For love and family today, be warm and kind. A kind word, a smile, or a small gift can bring you closer to the people you love.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time. Finish what matters most first, and ask for help when you need it.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
For money, keep things simple and careful today. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is brown. Wear something brown, or carry a small brown item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 5 to 7 in the morning. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the east. When you start something new, face the east, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 8, 22, and 283. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 8, 22, 283!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Pig's lucky days of the week are Thursday and Sunday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Pig can be overly trusting or indulgent. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: brown. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 8, 22, and 283.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

```
4

5
00:00:12,276 --> 00:00:14,503

6

នេះជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ។

7
00:00:17,967 --> 00:00:19,950
ថ្ងៃនេះ គឺជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក!

8
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,316

9
00:00:22,366 --> 00:00:24,683

10
00:00:24,733 --> 00:00:26,820
សំណាងល្អ និងគ្រួសារដ៏រីករាយ។

11

12

13
00:00:32,793 --> 00:00:34,274
សន្ដោស និងសុខដុម។

14
00:00:34,324 --> 00:00:37,788
កុរឱ្យតម្លៃលើភាពផាសុកភាព សេចក្តីសប្បុរស

15
00:00:37,838 --> 00:00:39,950
និងទំនាក់ទំនងស្មោះត្រង់។

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,172
សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ថ្ងៃនេះ

17
00:00:42,222 --> 00:00:44,172
សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំងណាស់។

18
00:00:44,222 --> 00:00:46,839
សូមរក្សាចិត្តឲ្យស្ងប់ ទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ

19
00:00:46,889 --> 00:00:49,950
ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

20
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,069
សម្រាប់ស្នេហា និងគ្រួសារ ថ្ងៃនេះ

21
00:00:52,119 --> 00:00:54,056
សូមមានចិត្តកក់ក្ដៅ និងសប្បុរស។

22
00:00:54,106 --> 00:00:56,440
ពាក្យសម្ដីល្អ ស្នាមញញឹម ឬអំណោយតូចមួយ

23
00:00:56,490 --> 00:00:58,361
អាចនាំឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែជិតស្និទ្ធ

24
00:00:58,411 --> 00:00:59,950
នឹងមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់។

25
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,905

26

សូមធ្វើម្ដងមួយជំហាន ឲ្យបានច្បាស់លាស់។

27
00:01:04,737 --> 00:01:06,642
សូមធ្វើរឿងសំខាន់បំផុតមុនគេ

28

29

សម្រាប់លុយកាក់ ថ្ងៃនេះ

30
00:01:11,982 --> 00:01:15,175
សូមចាយវាយឲ្យសាមញ្ញ និងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

31

សូមគិតគូរមុននឹងចាយ

33

34

35
00:01:26,325 --> 00:01:29,950
ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

36
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,357
ពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ

37
00:01:32,407 --> 00:01:34,672

38

39

ការជួបជុំ ឬការចាប់ផ្ដើមថ្មី។

40

41
00:01:42,951 --> 00:01:46,589

43

44

47

50

52

53

54

55
00:02:22,564 --> 00:02:26,446
ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

56
00:02:26,496 --> 00:02:29,950

57

មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់

58

សូមចំណាយពេលស្ងាត់មួយភ្លែត ដើម្បីអរគុណ។

59

អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក ហើយអរគុណខ្លួនឯង

60

ដែលបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

61
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,630
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

62

ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។

63
00:02:43,200 --> 00:02:45,630
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

64

65

66
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

67
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373

00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232

69
00:02:56,282 --> 00:02:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។
```

A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's and the animals' pictures added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

Premium 3D animated fantasy style inspired by classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films, golden zodiac characters with polished gold textures, engraved ornaments and pink-and-gold accents, oversized sparkling eyes and very expressive faces. Background: a softly glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under a huge full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pond, magical golden sparkles. Bright, high-contrast, saturated colors that stand out on a small phone screen; strong rim light separating the characters from the background; slightly blurred background so the faces pop. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). No watermark, no logo, no YouTube buttons or arrows. Twelve cute golden zodiac animals in a tight arc across the frame, all looking at the camera with excited faces; three of them shine much brighter with golden auras, one rests calmly in a soft blue bubble of light. Large bold text, at most a few words, in thick rounded sans-serif capitals with a dark outline and a soft drop shadow, perfectly spelled: "ALL 12 SIGNS" and "TODAY". The text sits across the top of the frame in the dark sky, never over a face.

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-28-long.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:03,243
Three signs are lucky today

2
00:00:03,293 --> 00:00:06,657
and one should take it easy.

3
00:00:06,707 --> 00:00:09,950
Where does your sign stand?

4
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:12,488
Today, Wednesday, 28 October 2026

5
00:00:12,538 --> 00:00:13,488
is a Pig day.

6
00:00:13,538 --> 00:00:16,181
It is a Great Day for everyone born

7
00:00:16,231 --> 00:00:17,950
in the Year of the Pig.

8
00:00:18,000 --> 00:00:19,950
This is your day to shine!

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,033
Generous and warm-hearted

10
00:00:22,083 --> 00:00:24,367
the Pig symbolizes abundance

11
00:00:24,417 --> 00:00:26,950
good fortune, and a happy home.

12
00:00:27,000 --> 00:00:29,950
Today, this good energy is with you.

13
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,814
Today's luckiest signs

14
00:00:31,864 --> 00:00:34,272
are the Pig, Rabbit and Goat.

15
00:00:34,322 --> 00:00:36,475
Snake, take it easy today.

16
00:00:36,525 --> 00:00:39,950
```

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden pig bring the light back before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the moon lantern grew dim. Its light was almost gone. Without it, tonight's full-moon festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden pig. Today was a Pig day, and the pig felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little frog named Kong came hopping over. "Little Pig," he said, "only you can bring the light back. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Generous and warm-hearted, the Pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune, and a happy home. Kong smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To relight the moon lantern, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the pig, and it set off on its adventure, with Kong right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Pig are warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing. Pigs value comfort, kindness, and honest relationships.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was brown, the pig's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The pig tied a brown ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Kong, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the east, the pig's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But a wide river blocked the path, with only small stepping stones to cross it. The pig stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Pig can be overly trusting or indulgent.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Kong came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Stone by stone, the pig hopped across the river. Each stone glowed gold under its feet. On the other side, it laughed with joy.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Kong. For the Pig today, that is from 5 to 7 in the morning. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the pig rang the golden bells 8 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The pig climbed 22 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a shining pearl of moonlight. It was the heart of the moon lantern!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the pearl, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Thursday and Sunday shone brightest, the Pig's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the pearl carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Kong right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The pig placed the pearl inside the moon lantern. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, whoosh! A golden light burst into the sky!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The lotus pond glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The pig felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Pig, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: brown. Best time: from 5 to 7 in the morning. Lucky direction: east. Lucky numbers: 8, 22, and 283.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Kong fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

### Khmer
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
តើជ្រូកមាសតូចមួយ អាចនាំពន្លឺត្រឡប់មកវិញ មុនព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់រះ បានទេ? កាលពីព្រេងនាយ នៅក្បែរប្រាសាទបុរាណមួយ ក្នុងប្រទេសកម្ពុជា រឿងរបស់យើងចាប់ផ្ដើម។

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
ប៉ុន្តែព្រឹកនេះ ចង្កៀងនៃព្រះចន្ទ ស្រអាប់ទៅវិញ។ ពន្លឺរបស់វា ស្ទើរតែរលត់។ បើគ្មានវា ពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់យប់នេះ មិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានទេ។

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក ហើយមានជ្រូកមាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។ ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំកុរ ហើយជ្រូកមានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
កង្កែបតូចមួយ ឈ្មោះគង់ លោតតម្រង់មក។ គង់និយាយថា "ជ្រូកតូចអើយ មានតែអ្នកទេ ដែលអាចនាំពន្លឺត្រឡប់មកវិញ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
ជ្រូក មានចិត្តទូលាយ និងកក់ក្ដៅ។ ឆ្នាំកុរតំណាងឲ្យភាពសម្បូរសប្បាយ សំណាងល្អ និងគ្រួសារដ៏រីករាយ។ គង់ញញឹម។ នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
ដើម្បីបំភ្លឺចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទឡើងវិញ វីរបុរសតូច ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
ជ្រូកនិយាយថា "តោះទៅ!" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ជាមនុស្សកក់ក្តៅ សន្ដោស និងសុខដុម។ កុរឱ្យតម្លៃលើភាពផាសុកភាព សេចក្តីសប្បុរស និងទំនាក់ទំនងស្មោះត្រង់។

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។ មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។ វាគឺពណ៌ត្នោត ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ជ្រូកថ្ងៃនេះ។

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
ជ្រូកចងខ្សែបូពណ៌ត្នោត ជុំវិញក។ គង់និយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។"

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។ ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។ វាចង្អុលទៅទិសខាងកើត ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ជ្រូក។

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
ប៉ុន្តែទន្លេធំមួយ បាំងផ្លូវ ហើយមានតែដុំថ្មតូចៗ សម្រាប់ឈានឆ្លង។ ជ្រូកឈប់។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ជួនកាលជឿគេពេក ឬធ្វេសប្រហែសខ្លួនឯង។

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
គង់មកជិត ហើយញញឹម។ គង់និយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។ ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។ អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។"

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
ម្ដងមួយដុំៗ ជ្រូកលោតឆ្លងទន្លេ។ ដុំថ្មនីមួយៗ ភ្លឺពណ៌មាស នៅក្រោមជើងរបស់វា។ នៅម្ខាងទៀត វាសើចយ៉ាងសប្បាយ។

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។ តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។ នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
គង់និយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។" សម្រាប់ជ្រូកថ្ងៃនេះ គឺម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ជ្រូកវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស ចំនួនប្រាំបី ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។ ទីង! ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។ ជ្រូកឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួនម្ភៃពីរ កាំ ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ ពួកគេបានរកឃើញគុជពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទ ភ្លឺចែងចាំង។ វាគឺជាបេះដូង នៃចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
នៅជុំវិញគុជ មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។ ថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំកុរ។

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។ វីរបុរសតូច កាន់គុជយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរ។

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។ យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។ តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
ជ្រូកដាក់គុជចូលក្នុងចង្កៀងព្រះចន្ទ។ មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។ បន្ទាប់មក វឺត! ពន្លឺមាសមួយ បាញ់ឡើងទៅលើមេឃ!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
ស្រះផ្កាឈូក ភ្លឺចែងចាំងម្ដងទៀត។ គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។ ជ្រូកមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។ វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។ ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។ ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។ ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោងប្រាំ ដល់ម៉ោងប្រាំពីរ ព្រឹក។ ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។ លេខសំណាង៖ លេខប្រាំបី លេខម្ភៃពីរ និងលេខពីររយប៉ែតសិបបី។

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងគង់ ក៏លង់លក់ ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)

```
00:04:13,145 --> 00:04:14,950
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

101
00:04:15,000 --> 00:04:17,853
ជ្រូកមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

102
00:04:17,903 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

103
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,916
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

104
00:04:23,966 --> 00:04:26,588
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

105
00:04:26,638 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

106
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,630
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

107
00:04:31,680 --> 00:04:33,150
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ត្នោត។

108
00:04:33,200 --> 00:04:35,630
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

109
00:04:35,680 --> 00:04:37,230
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងកើត។

110
00:04:37,280 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 8 លេខ 22 និងលេខ 283។

111
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,155
ហើយដូច្នេះ នៅក្រោមពន្លឺព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់

112
00:04:44,205 --> 00:04:47,450
វីរបុរសមាសតូច និងគង់ ក៏លង់លក់

113
00:04:47,500 --> 00:04:49,950
ដោយសុបិនអំពីថ្ងៃស្អែក។

114
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:52,258
នេះគឺជាជោគជតារាសីចិន សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនេះ។

115
00:04:52,308 --> 00:04:54,373
អ្នកអាចមើលរាសីទាំង 12 និងលេខសំណាង

116
00:04:54,423 --> 00:04:56,232
នៅលើគេហទំព័រ mybirthsign.com។

117
00:04:56,282 --> 00:04:58,540
សូមតាមដានយើង សម្រាប់ជោគជតាថ្ងៃស្អែក។

118
00:04:58,590 --> 00:04:59,950
សូមឲ្យអ្នកមានសំណាងល្អ!
```

### Subtitles – English (2026-10-28-story.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,778

2
00:00:02,828 --> 00:00:04,984

3
00:00:05,034 --> 00:00:07,881
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,931 --> 00:00:09,950

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,240
But this morning

6
00:00:11,290 --> 00:00:13,337

7
00:00:13,387 --> 00:00:15,434

8
00:00:15,484 --> 00:00:17,934

9

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,965
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus

11
00:00:23,015 --> 00:00:25,979

12
00:00:26,029 --> 00:00:27,376
Today was a Pig day

13
00:00:27,426 --> 00:00:29,950
and the pig felt something special.

14
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,495
A little frog named

15
00:00:31,545 --> 00:00:33,365

16
00:00:33,415 --> 00:00:35,072

17
00:00:35,122 --> 00:00:37,917

00:00:43,813 --> 00:00:45,993

22
00:00:46,043 --> 00:00:46,856
Kong smiled.

23
00:00:46,906 --> 00:00:48,295
That was exactly why

24
00:00:48,345 --> 00:00:49,950

25
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,110
To relight the moon lantern

26
00:00:52,160 --> 00:00:54,350

27
00:00:54,400 --> 00:00:56,910

28
00:00:56,960 --> 00:00:59,950

29
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,842

31
00:01:06,627 --> 00:01:09,950

32

33

34
00:01:15,891 --> 00:01:17,237
Pigs value comfort

35
00:01:17,287 --> 00:01:19,950

36
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,223

37
00:01:22,273 --> 00:01:24,874

38
00:01:24,924 --> 00:01:26,768

39
00:01:26,818 --> 00:01:29,950
It was brown, the pig's lucky color today.

40
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,736
The pig tied a brown

41
00:01:31,786 --> 00:01:33,789

42
00:01:33,839 --> 00:01:36,825
"Wear your lucky color," said Kong

43
00:01:36,875 --> 00:01:39,950

44

45
00:01:42,627 --> 00:01:43,933

46
00:01:43,983 --> 00:01:45,882

47
00:01:45,932 --> 00:01:47,747

48
00:01:47,797 --> 00:01:49,950

49
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,858

50
00:01:51,908 --> 00:01:53,245

51
00:01:53,295 --> 00:01:54,343

52
00:01:54,393 --> 00:01:55,268
The pig stopped.

53
00:01:55,318 --> 00:01:57,638
Sometimes, people born in the Year of the

54

56

59

61

62
00:02:16,855 --> 00:02:19,950

63

64

65
00:02:26,639 --> 00:02:29,950

66
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,347
At last, they reached a great

67

68

69

70

"The door opens only

71

75

77

78

79

80

81

82

88

89

90
00:03:25,758 --> 00:03:28,359

91
00:03:28,409 --> 00:03:29,950

92
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:32,009

93
00:03:32,059 --> 00:03:34,656

94
00:03:34,706 --> 00:03:37,401

95
00:03:37,451 --> 00:03:39,950

96
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774

97
00:03:43,824 --> 00:03:45,538

98
00:03:45,588 --> 00:03:48,381

99
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950

100
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,855

101

102

103

00:03:57,302 --> 00:03:59,950

106
```

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Snake (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Snake, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Snake clashes with the Pig day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add the reference pictures of Chanda and the Snake as ingredients (the Snake is the only animal in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the snake hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little lotus lantern keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Stop the scroll
   English: Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Snake? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.
   Khmer: ឈប់! កុំអូសរំលង។ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ រាសីអ្នកថ្ងៃនេះមិនល្អទេ។ តែកុំបារម្ភ សូមមើលឲ្យចប់។
2. Warning
   English: Year of the Snake, wait! Today is a clash day for you. Here's how to get through it calmly.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ចាំបន្តិច! ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃប៉ះឆុងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលវិធីឆ្លងកាត់ថ្ងៃនេះដោយស្ងប់។
3. Question
   English: Does everything feel a bit off today? If you were born in the Year of the Snake, here's why.
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ អ្វីៗហាក់ដូចជាមិនសូវស្រួលមែនទេ? បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ នេះជាមូលហេតុ។
4. Send it to a friend
   English: Know someone born in the Year of the Snake? Send this to them before they make a big decision today.
   Khmer: ស្គាល់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ទេ? ផ្ញើវីដេអូនេះទៅគាត់ មុនពេលគាត់សម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។
5. Only one sign
   English: Out of all twelve signs, only one should slow down today: the Snake.
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗថ្ងៃនេះ គឺឆ្នាំម្សាញ់។
★ = used to open this video. Why: it stops the scroll by speaking straight to people of this sign, and the promise "watch to the end" keeps them for the gentle advice that follows. To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with big warm amber-brown eyes, rosy cheeks and a bright open smile, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun with a golden lotus hairpin and dangling gold chains, small gold flower earrings, a cream-and-gold brocade Khmer blouse with a high collar, puffed shoulders and three-quarter sleeves, a gold chain necklace with a large round golden medallion showing the twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a flower buckle and a red gem, and a deep navy sampot skirt woven with gold Khmer diamond patterns and a front pleated panel, stands on the carved stone temple terrace above the moonlit lotus pond, facing the camera and speaking warmly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. She starts by raising one open hand toward the camera like a friendly "stop" sign, with a caring, slightly urgent expression, then lowers it and keeps talking warmly. ## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Snake? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 28 October, is a Pig day, opposite the Snake in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can be secretive or prone to overthinking. Pause before you react.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។ មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។ ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។ រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
យប់នេះ សូមសម្រាកឲ្យស្រួល។ ថ្ងៃស្អែក ជាថ្ងៃថ្មី ហើយអាចជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នក។ សូមតាមដាន MyBirthSign រៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Pig) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Rabbit and Goat), the sign to take it easy (Snake) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 character pictures as ingredients in Flow (each part uses its own animal). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Pig
2:40 Good Day: Rabbit
3:30 Good Day: Goat
4:20 Take it easy: Snake
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Snake? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំម្សាញ់មែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Pig has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំកុរមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video. Why: in one line it gives every viewer a stake (a Great Day, a Good Day, or a day to slow down) and ends on a question, so people stay to find their own sign. To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's and the animals' pictures added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)
Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with big warm amber-brown eyes, rosy cheeks and a bright open smile, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun with a golden lotus hairpin and dangling gold chains, small gold flower earrings, a cream-and-gold brocade Khmer blouse with a high collar, puffed shoulders and three-quarter sleeves, a gold chain necklace with a large round golden medallion showing the twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a flower buckle and a red gem, and a deep navy sampot skirt woven with gold Khmer diamond patterns and a front pleated panel, stands on the moonlit temple terrace beside the lotus pond, facing the camera, smiling warmly and speaking directly to the viewer with natural lip movements and small, friendly hand gestures. Behind her, twelve cute golden zodiac animals sit on glowing lotus pedestals in a half circle: the pig shines brightest with a golden aura, and the snake rests calmly inside a soft bubble of golden light. The animals move gently and look around curiously, mouths closed. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward her face. ## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today is a Pig day. Coming up: one Great Day sign, two Good Days, one sign to take it easy, and all twelve.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Pig, Rabbit and Goat are lucky today, and the Snake should slow down. Find your sign in the chapters below.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Wednesday, 28 October: a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Pig!

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Generous and warm-hearted, the Pig symbolizes abundance, good fortune, and a happy home.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
People born in the Year of the Pig are often described as warm-hearted, generous, and easygoing.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Your luck is strong today. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
For love today, be warm and kind. A kind word or a small gift brings you closer.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
For work and study, take one clear step at a time, and finish what matters most first.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
For money, keep it simple. Plan before you spend, and save a little for tomorrow.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
Your lucky color today is brown. Wear it, or carry something brown with you.

Your best time is from 5 to 7 in the morning, and your lucky direction is the east.

The Pig's lucky days of the week are Thursday and Sunday. Keep them for plans that matter.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)

```
មុននឹងចាប់ផ្ដើម សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក

12
00:00:33,956 --> 00:00:36,873
ក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយ subscribe

13
00:00:36,923 --> 00:00:39,950
ដើម្បីទទួលជោគជតារៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។

14
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:43,760
ថ្ងៃពុធ ថ្ងៃទី 28 ខែតុលា

15
00:00:43,810 --> 00:00:49,950
ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង សម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ!

16
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,394
ជ្រូក មានចិត្តទូលាយ និងកក់ក្ដៅ។

17
00:00:53,444 --> 00:00:56,839
ឆ្នាំកុរតំណាងឲ្យភាពសម្បូរសប្បាយ

18
00:00:56,889 --> 00:00:59,950
សំណាងល្អ និងគ្រួសារដ៏រីករាយ។

19
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:06,162
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ ជារឿយៗ ត្រូវបានគេស្គាល់ថា

20
00:01:06,212 --> 00:01:09,950
កក់ក្តៅ សន្ដោស និងសុខដុម។

21
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:14,379
ថ្ងៃនេះ សំណាងរបស់អ្នកល្អខ្លាំង។

22
00:01:14,429 --> 00:01:19,950
សូមទទួលយកឱកាសល្អៗ ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ។

23
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:24,717
សម្រាប់ស្នេហា ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមានចិត្តកក់ក្ដៅ។

24
00:01:24,767 --> 00:01:29,950
ពាក្យល្អ ឬអំណោយតូចមួយ នាំឲ្យកាន់តែជិតស្និទ្ធ។

25
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:33,664
សម្រាប់ការងារ និងការសិក្សា

26
00:01:33,714 --> 00:01:39,950
សូមធ្វើម្ដងមួយជំហាន ហើយបញ្ចប់រឿងសំខាន់មុនគេ។

27
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:43,924
សម្រាប់លុយកាក់ សូមចាយឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

28
00:01:43,974 --> 00:01:47,771
គិតមុននឹងចាយ ហើយសន្សំទុកបន្តិច

29
00:01:47,821 --> 00:01:49,950
សម្រាប់ថ្ងៃស្អែក។

30
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:54,305
ពណ៌សំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ គឺពណ៌ត្នោត។

31
00:01:54,355 --> 00:01:59,950
សូមស្លៀក ឬយករបស់ពណ៌ត្នោត ជាប់ខ្លួន។

32
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:05,882
ពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក

33
00:02:05,932 --> 00:02:09,950
ហើយទិសសំណាង គឺទិសខាងកើត។

34
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:12,899
លេខសំណាង៖ 8 22 និង 283។

35
00:02:12,949 --> 00:02:17,258
សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នក ក្នុងមតិយោបល់

36
00:02:17,308 --> 00:02:19,950
ហើយរក្សាទុកវីដេអូនេះ!

37
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:22,575
ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំកុរ

38
00:02:22,625 --> 00:02:26,575
គឺថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍ និងថ្ងៃអាទិត្យ។

39
00:02:26,625 --> 00:02:29,950
សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។

40
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:33,494
រំលឹកបន្តិច៖ អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ

41
00:02:33,544 --> 00:02:37,798
ជួនកាលជឿគេពេក ឬធ្វេសប្រហែសខ្លួនឯង។

42
00:02:37,848 --> 00:02:39,950
សូមរក្សាតុល្យភាព។

43
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:43,561
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំថោះ៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

44
00:02:43,611 --> 00:02:49,950
ឆ្នាំថោះ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំកុរ។

45
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:54,771
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមសហការជាមួយមិត្ត។

46
00:02:54,821 --> 00:02:59,950
ផែនការរួមគ្នា នឹងដំណើរការល្អ។

47
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,398
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ផ្កាឈូក

48
00:03:03,448 --> 00:03:09,950
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 1 ដល់ម៉ោង 3 រសៀល។

49
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:14,474
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសនិរតី។

50
00:03:14,524 --> 00:03:19,950
លេខសំណាង៖ 3 32 និង 364។

51
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:22,976
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំថោះ ថ្ងៃនេះ

52
00:03:23,026 --> 00:03:26,134
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ។

53
00:03:26,184 --> 00:03:29,950
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

54
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:33,561
បន្ទាប់ ឆ្នាំមមែ៖ ថ្ងៃល្អ!

55
00:03:33,611 --> 00:03:39,950
ឆ្នាំមមែ ស្ថិតក្នុងត្រីកោណសំណាង ជាមួយឆ្នាំកុរ។

56
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,104
ថ្ងៃនេះ ល្អសម្រាប់ជួបមនុស្ស

57
00:03:44,154 --> 00:03:47,181
និងចាប់ផ្ដើមរឿងល្អៗ។

58
00:03:47,231 --> 00:03:49,950
សូមរក្សាស្នាមញញឹម។

59
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,108
ពណ៌សំណាងគឺពណ៌ក្រហម

60
00:03:53,158 --> 00:03:59,950
ហើយពេលល្អបំផុតគឺម៉ោង 5 ដល់ម៉ោង 7 ព្រឹក។

61
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:04,601
ទិសសំណាងគឺទិសខាងកើត។

62
00:04:04,651 --> 00:04:09,950
លេខសំណាង៖ 7 77 និង 217។

63
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:12,976
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំមមែ ថ្ងៃនេះ

64
00:04:13,026 --> 00:04:16,134
សូមនៅជិតអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំកុរ។

65
00:04:16,184 --> 00:04:19,950
សំណាងកាន់តែច្រើន ពេលចែករំលែក។

66
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,495
ថ្ងៃនេះ ថ្ងៃទី 28 ខែតុលា ជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំកុរ

67
00:04:23,545 --> 00:04:26,586
ទល់មុខឆ្នាំម្សាញ់ ក្នុងរង្វង់រាសី។

68
00:04:26,636 --> 00:04:29,950
មានថ្ងៃខ្លះ អ្វីៗមិនដូចការគ្រោងទុកទេ។

69
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,090
ហើយវាមិនអីទេ។

70
00:04:31,140 --> 00:04:34,248
មនុស្សគ្រប់គ្នា ធ្លាប់មានថ្ងៃបែបនេះ។

71
00:04:34,298 --> 00:04:37,494
ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ មិនមែនជាថ្ងៃអាក្រក់ទេ

72
00:04:37,544 --> 00:04:39,950
គឺជាថ្ងៃសម្រាប់ដកដង្ហើមវែងៗ។

73
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:42,386
សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖

74
00:04:42,436 --> 00:04:46,488
ជួនកាលសម្ងាត់ខ្លួន ឬគិតច្រើនពេក។

75
00:04:46,538 --> 00:04:49,950
សូមឈប់បន្តិច មុននឹងឆ្លើយតប។

76
00:04:50,000 --> 00:04:54,394
សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

77
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

78
00:04:56,420 --> 00:04:59,950
ហើយពឹងលើមិត្តដែលអ្នកទុកចិត្ត។

79
00:05:00,000 --> 00:05:02,422
ក្នុងការងារ និងលុយកាក់

80
00:05:02,472 --> 00:05:06,130
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

81
00:05:06,180 --> 00:05:09,950
បញ្ចប់រឿងមួយ រួចសឹមធ្វើរឿងបន្ទាប់។

82
00:05:10,000 --> 00:05:12,705
យឺត ក៏នៅតែជាការឈានទៅមុខដែរ។

83
00:05:12,755 --> 00:05:17,093
រាល់ថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ ធ្វើឲ្យអ្នកកាន់តែរឹងមាំ

84
00:05:17,143 --> 00:05:19,950
ហើយថ្ងៃភ្លឺស្វាង កំពុងមកដល់។

85
00:05:20,000 --> 00:05:22,993
ឆ្នាំជូត៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

86
00:05:23,043 --> 00:05:25,457
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ខៀវ។

87
00:05:25,507 --> 00:05:29,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 7 ដល់ម៉ោង 9 ព្រឹក។

88
00:05:30,000 --> 00:05:32,242
ជាថ្ងៃធម្មតា និងនឹងនរ។

89
00:05:32,292 --> 00:05:34,221
សូមធ្វើការដោយស្ងប់ៗ

90
00:05:34,271 --> 00:05:37,658
ហើយបញ្ចប់អ្វីដែលអ្នកបានចាប់ផ្ដើម។

91
00:05:37,708 --> 00:05:39,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

92
00:05:40,000 --> 00:05:43,144
ឆ្នាំឆ្លូវ៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

93
00:05:43,194 --> 00:05:45,506
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

94
00:05:45,556 --> 00:05:49,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 9 ដល់ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក។

95
00:05:50,000 --> 00:05:53,398
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមធ្វើអ្វីៗឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

96
00:05:53,448 --> 00:05:57,421
ជំហានតូចៗ និងនឹងនរ ល្អជាងការលោតធំៗ។

97
00:05:57,471 --> 00:05:59,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសអាគ្នេយ៍។

98
00:06:00,000 --> 00:06:02,575
ឆ្នាំខាល៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

99
00:06:02,625 --> 00:06:05,325
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ទឹកក្រូច។

100
00:06:05,375 --> 00:06:09,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោង 1 រសៀល។

101
00:06:10,000 --> 00:06:13,283
ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ សម្រាប់រៀបចំផែនការ។

102
00:06:13,333 --> 00:06:15,536
សូមរៀបចំរបស់របរ គិតទុកមុន

103
00:06:15,586 --> 00:06:17,968
និងសម្រាកឲ្យបានល្អនៅយប់នេះ។

104
00:06:18,018 --> 00:06:19,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។

105
00:06:20,000 --> 00:06:22,608
ឆ្នាំរោង៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

106
00:06:22,658 --> 00:06:25,140
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ប្រាក់។

107
00:06:25,190 --> 00:06:29,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 យប់ ដល់ម៉ោង 1 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

108
00:06:30,000 --> 00:06:32,290
ជាថ្ងៃធម្មតា និងនឹងនរ។

109
00:06:32,340 --> 00:06:34,312
សូមធ្វើការដោយស្ងប់ៗ

110
00:06:34,362 --> 00:06:37,822
ហើយបញ្ចប់អ្វីដែលអ្នកបានចាប់ផ្ដើម។

111
00:06:37,872 --> 00:06:39,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងជើង។

112
00:06:40,000 --> 00:06:42,908
ឆ្នាំមមី៖ ថ្ងៃធម្មតា។

113
00:06:42,958 --> 00:06:45,302
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌លឿង។

114
00:06:45,352 --> 00:06:49,950
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 3 ដល់ម៉ោង 5 ទៀបភ្លឺ។

115
00:06:50,000 --> 00:06:53,564
ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមធ្វើអ្វីៗឲ្យសាមញ្ញ។

116
00:06:53,614 --> 00:06:57,781
ជំហានតូចៗ និងនឹងនរ ល្អជាងការលោតធំៗ។

117
00:06:57,831 --> 00:06:59,950
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសឦសាន។

118
```

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Premium 3D animated fantasy film quality, inspired by classic Disney and Pixar-style animated family films: cute and majestic golden zodiac characters with oversized sparkling eyes, adorable expressive faces, soft rounded features, polished gold textures with intricate engraved ornaments and delicate pink and gold accents, alive and emotionally expressive. ## Scene 1 – Pig day (Chanda on camera)
Chanda, a friendly young Cambodian woman presenter with big warm amber-brown eyes, rosy cheeks and a bright open smile, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun with a golden lotus hairpin and dangling gold chains, small gold flower earrings, a cream-and-gold brocade Khmer blouse with a high collar, puffed shoulders and three-quarter sleeves, a gold chain necklace with a large round golden medallion showing the twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a flower buckle and a red gem, and a deep navy sampot skirt woven with gold Khmer diamond patterns and a front pleated panel, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Chanda matches her reference image. Then: magical and powerful. A chubby, adorable golden piglet with big happy eyes and rosy cheeks stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it floats up from the lotus inside a glowing golden bubble, spinning happily as golden coins and petals swirl around it. The lotus pond ripples with golden light and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. A chubby, adorable golden piglet with big happy eyes and rosy cheeks, an adorable golden rabbit with long shining ears, big sparkling eyes and a tiny pearl necklace and an adorable golden goat with big soft eyes, curled shining horns and a tiny bell fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Snake, take it easy
Premium 3D animated golden style, same characters as the reference pictures. A cute, elegant golden snake with big shiny eyes and jewel-like patterned scales curls up on a lotus pedestal as a soft protective bubble of golden light forms around it like a shield. Gentle glowing butterflies and petals circle it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. The mood is peaceful, safe and magical, not sad. The camera moves in slowly. The snake matches the reference image.

## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. Golden light beams shoot up from the temple towers into the night sky and burst into gentle golden fireworks shaped like lotus flowers. Hundreds of floating lanterns rise over the glowing pond and the huge full moon. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.

## Scene 5 – Lucky numbers (about 10–12 seconds)
Magical and powerful. A huge glowing golden zodiac wheel rises out of the lotus pond in front of the temple, with twelve cute golden zodiac animals around its rim. It spins faster and faster, shooting beams of golden light and sparkles, then slows to a gentle, shining turn under the full moon. The camera slowly pushes in, keeping open space in the middle and lower part of the frame for the number captions. (Loop the slow-turning part, or slow-zoom a still picture, to fill the time.)

សម្រាប់លេខសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ឆ្នាំកុរម្ភៃពីរ និងពីររយប៉ែតសិបបី។ ឆ្នាំថោះសាមសិបពីរ និងបីរយហុកសិបបួន។ ឆ្នាំមមែចិតសិបប្រាំពីរ និងពីររយដប់ប្រាំពីរ។ សូមសរសេរលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នក នៅក្នុងមតិយោបល់ ហើយនិយាយម្ដងទៀត!
